# Pre-Greek features

[[wiki/pre-greek/_index|..]]

***

<!-- BEGIN kaphtor:record -->

The features are the numbered sections of Beekes 2014, chapters 2–3, by id and the affix or letters each treats; their headings are withheld. A word names a feature when its entry in the book's lexicon cites it, and is an example when the section cites the word.
